Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in automata theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 662 and the invariant I(A,B) = 418 mod 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_662(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 662. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^418(S, F) is trivial. QED.
